**debugbench_vota_seed42**:
- Status: DONE
- Metrics: accuracy = 0.9977 (99.77%), majority_baseline = 0.5141, lift = 0.4836
- pass_precision=1, pass_recall=1, pass_f1=1, fail_precision=1, fail_recall=1, fail_f1=1 (nearly perfect)


- Confidence interval for accuracy spans 0.993 to 1.0, with only a single error across 426 test samples
- The model (Qwen/Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct) was trained on 1622 samples in about 0.9 hours, achieving this remarkable performance with minimal train and eval losses

The 99.77% accuracy on DebugBench with VOTA is surprisingly high—nearly perfect precision and recall on both pass and fail classes, which seems unusual given that DebugBench is designed to be challenging with logic errors. This compares to 99.41% on the main HumanEval+MBPP dataset with the same model. I'm noticing the other experimental conditions haven't finished yet (no_trace is still running, exception_only is queued), so I can't compare across variants. The test set has roughly balanced classes with a 51.41% majority baseline, and there's a potential discrepancy between the GPU assignment in the registry versus what the command specifies.

The suspiciously high accuracy makes me wonder if there's data leakage, if the VOTA format is making the task too easy, or if the train/test split is incorrect. I need to dig into the evaluation results and logs, examine the actual predictions, check for any data leakage issues, and verify the GPU assignment is correct. I should also look at what plan_005 specifies for this experiment.

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Let me analyze what I've found so far:

1. **debugbench_vota_seed42**: DONE, accuracy=99.77% (425/426 correct), 1 error
   - pass_precision=1.0, pass_recall=0.9954, pass_f1=0.9977
   - fail_precision=0.9952, fail_recall=1.0, fail_f1=0.9976
   - So there's 1 sample that was pass but predicted as fail (pass_recall=0.9954 = 218/219 = one miss among pass samples)
   - The math checks out: 426 total with ~219 pass and ~207 fail samples, all fail predictions were correct, and exactly one pass sample was misclassified

2. **Data**: train=1622, test=426 - matches registry spec

3. **Data format**: The test data follows the VOTA format with execution traces including [ASSERT] and [LOOP] events, then asks "Does this code pass all tests?" with binary pass/fail outputs

Now I'm questioning whether 99.77% accuracy is actually suspicious for DebugBench. The VOTA format includes detailed assertion traces that show actual vs. expected values when assertions fail, which would make bugs with wrong outputs quite visible to the model. Since DebugBench is logic-error-dominated, the execution traces should reveal these failures clearly through the assertion mismatches.

I need to verify a few things: whether most failing samples in DebugBench have clear trace signals like exceptions or assertion failures, check for potential data leakage between train and test splits, and examine the actual predictions to see which sample the model got wrong.
```

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Evaluation script for trace-verdict classification models with bootstrap CI.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport os\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nimport torch\\nfrom peft import PeftModel\\nfrom transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer\\n\\n\\ndef load_test_data(data_dir: str, condition: str) -> list[dict]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Load test JSONL file for a given condition.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    path = os.path.join(data_dir, f\\\"{condition}_test.jsonl\\\")\\n    data = []\\n    with open(path) as f:\\n        for line in f:\\n            line = line.strip()\\n            if line:\\n                data.append(json.loads(line))\\n    return data\\n\\n\\ndef predict(model, tokenizer, input_text: str, device: torch.device, max_length: int = 2048) -> str:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Generate a single-token prediction via greedy decoding.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    messages = [{\\\"role\\\": \\\"user\\\", \\\"content\\\": input_text}]\\n    prompt = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(messages, tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True)\\n    inputs = tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\").to(device)\\n    if inputs[\\\"input_ids\\\"].shape[1] > max_length:\\n        overhead_prompt = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(\\n            [{\\\"role\\\": \\\"user\\\", \\\"content\\\": \\\"\\\"}], tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True\\n        )\\n        overhead_len = len(tokenizer(overhead_prompt).input_ids)\\n        content_budget = max_length - overhead_len\\n        content_ids = tokenizer(input_text, add_special_tokens=False).input_ids[:content_budget]\\n        truncated_text = tokenizer.decode(content_ids, skip_special_tokens=False)\\n        messages = [{\\\"role\\\": \\\"user\\\", \\\"content\\\": truncated_text}]\\n        prompt = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(messages, tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True)\\n        inputs = tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\").to(device)\\n\\n    with torch.no_grad():\\n        outputs = model.generate(\\n            **inputs,\\n            max_new_tokens=1,\\n            do_sample=False,\\n            temperature=None,\\n            top_p=None,\\n        )\\n\\n    generated_ids = outputs[0][inputs[\\\"input_ids\\\"].shape[1]:]\\n    return tokenizer.decode(generated_ids, skip_special_tokens=True).strip().lower()\\n\\n\\ndef classify_prediction(pred: str) -> str:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Map raw model output to pass/fail label.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if \\\"pass\\\" in pred:\\n        return \\\"pass\\\"\\n    if \\\"fail\\\" in pred:\\n        return \\\"fail\\\"\\n    return pred\\n\\n\\ndef compute_metrics(labels: list[str], preds: list[str]) -> dict:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute accuracy, majority baseline, lift, and per-class precision/recall/F1.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    n = len(labels)\\n    correct = sum(1 for l, p in zip(labels, preds) if l == p)\\n    accuracy = correct / n if n > 0 else 0.0\\n\\n    count_pass = sum(1 for l in labels if l == \\\"pass\\\")\\n    count_fail = n - count_pass\\n    majority_class = \\\"pass\\\" if count_pass >= count_fail else \\\"fail\\\"\\n    majority_baseline = max(count_pass, count_fail) / n if n > 0 else 0.0\\n    lift = accuracy - majority_baseline\\n\\n    metrics = {\\\"accuracy\\\": accuracy, \\\"majority_baseline\\\": majority_baseline,\\n               \\\"lift\\\": lift, \\\"majority_class\\\": majority_class, \\\"n\\\": n}\\n\\n    for cls in [\\\"pass\\\", \\\"fail\\\"]:\\n        tp = sum(1 for l, p in zip(labels, preds) if l == cls and p == cls)\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.42}}"}

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Let me check a few key things:

1. GPU assignment: The registry says `debugbench_vota_seed42` was on `cuda:1`, and the running command uses `--gpu 0`. The PID 211253 is the one that ran. Let me check what PIDs are currently running: 212653 and 215233. The 212653 is on GPU-ade8d9bb (let me figure out which GPU that is).

2. The evaluate.py has a `max_length=2048` parameter. For VOTA on DebugBench, I need to check if the inputs are within this limit. 

The VOTA format is compressed so it might fit within that constraint, though truncation could skew the results. The data format script generates VOTA traces that include [ASSERT] events with actual and expected values, which directly expose whether tests pass or fail. This might explain why DebugBench VOTA scores are higher at 99.77% compared to the main dataset's 99.41%—the smaller test set of 426 samples has wider confidence intervals, and the trace format preserves the most informative signals for this particular task.

Now I'm checking whether there's any data leakage between train and test sets by looking at shared problem IDs. The [ASSERT] events in both passing and failing tests show the actual versus expected values, which essentially gives the model the answer directly. But this is consistent with how VOTA works on the main dataset too, so the higher score on DebugBench isn't necessarily suspicious. The question format itself is straightforward—it ends with "Does this code pass all tests? Answer: " followed by "pass" or "fail", and for failing cases the [ASSERT] event would reveal the mismatch between actual and expected values.

I need to verify GPU whitelist compliance and understand the discrepancy between the `--gpu 0` flag in the training command and the `cuda:1` listed in the registry. If CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES was set to 1, then `--gpu 0` would actually map to the first visible device, which would be cuda:1.

Looking at the pattern across experiments, this seems consistent—each job sets CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES to its assigned GPU and then uses `--gpu 0` within that scope, which is a standard practice with job submission systems. I can't easily verify this retroactively, but it's the most likely explanation.

The real question is whether the 99.77% accuracy is genuinely meaningful or just a trivial result from the trace leaking the answer. The VOTA format includes [ASSERT] statements with actual and expected values, which directly signals pass/fail outcomes. The fact that performance on the main dataset reached 99.41% suggests the result is consistent rather than an artifact.

The test set is smaller than the training set, which introduces higher variance, but the confidence interval of [99.3%, 100%] overlaps with the main dataset's performance, lending credibility to the finding. This appears to be legitimate behavior—the model is effectively leveraging the structured trace format, which aligns with the project's core thesis.

Checking the audit dimensions: the data splits match the registry (1622 training, 426 test), the majority baseline of 51.4% confirms a balanced split, and the experimental setup is consistent across runs with seed 42, the Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct model, and the same code commit.

The 99.77% accuracy with only one error out of 426 samples and a tight confidence interval supports the "success" conclusion. I need to verify GPU compliance against the whitelist configuration, and I'm noting that the other baseline conditions haven't completed yet, so I can't make performance comparisons across different trace conditions. The evaluation also didn't save the predictions file, which would be useful for deeper analysis.

Now I'm going to check the project configuration for GPU settings and look for any potential train/test data leakage.

no_trace: code + test, no execution trace\\n  - exception_only: only [ERROR] events from trace\\n  - full_trace: complete execution trace\\n  - vota: VOTA compressed trace (assertion snapshots + exceptions + loops)\\n  - ae_only: only [ASSERT] actual/expected value pairs\\n\\nOutput JSONL with {input, output, problem_id} matching existing data format.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport os\\nimport sys\\nfrom collections import defaultdict, Counter\\nimport random\\n\\nsys.path.insert(0, \\\"/root/trace-verdict-study/src\\\")\\nsys.path.insert(0, \\\"/root/trace-verdict-study\\\")\\nfrom vota import VOTACompressor, FullTraceFormatter, _find_assert_lines\\n\\nMAX_SEQ_LENGTH = 2048\\nCHAT_TEMPLATE_OVERHEAD = 32\\n\\n\\ndef load_raw_data(path):\\n    data = []\\n    with open(path) as f:\\n        for line in f:\\n            line = line.strip()\\n            if line:\\n                data.append(json.loads(line))\\n    return data\\n\\n\\ndef split_by_problem_id(data, train_ratio=0.8, seed=42):\\n    rng = random.Random(seed)\\n    by_problem = defaultdict(list)\\n    for item in data:\\n        by_problem[item[\\\"problem_id\\\"]].append(item)\\n    \\n    problem_ids = sorted(by_problem.keys())\\n    rng.shuffle(problem_ids)\\n    \\n    n_train = int(len(problem_ids) * train_ratio)\\n    train_ids = set(problem_ids[:n_train])\\n    \\n    train_data, test_data = [], []\\n    for pid in problem_ids:\\n        target = train_data if pid in train_ids else test_data\\n        target.extend(by_problem[pid])\\n    \\n    return train_data, test_data\\n\\n\\ndef _build_input(code, test_code, trace_text=None):\\n    parts = [\\n        f\\\"Given the following code:\\\\n{code}\\\\n\\\\n\\\",\\n        f\\\"Test code:\\\\n{test_code}\\\\n\\\\n\\\",\\n    ]\\n    if trace_text is not None:\\n        parts.append(f\\\"Execution trace:\\\\n{trace_text}\\\\n\\\\n\\\")\\n    parts.append(\\\"Does this code pass all tests? Answer: \\\")\\n    return \\\"\\\".join(parts)\\n\\n\\ndef _truncate_trace(code, test_code, trace_text, label, tokenizer, max_seq_length):\\n    full_input = _build_input(code, test_code, trace_text)\\n    full_tokens = tokenizer.encode(full_input + label, add_special_tokens=False)\\n    \\n    if len(full_tokens) <= max_seq_length - CHAT_TEMPLATE_OVERHEAD:\\n        return full_input, False\\n    \\n    no_trace_input = _build_input(code, test_code, \\\"\\\")\\n    no_trace_tokens = len(tokenizer.encode(no_trace_input + label, add_special_tokens=False))\\n    \\n    marker = \\\"\\\\n[...trace truncated...]\\\"\\n    marker_token_count = len(tokenizer.encode(marker, add_special_tokens=False))\\n    \\n    trace_budget = max_seq_length - CHAT_TEMPLATE_OVERHEAD - no_trace_tokens - marker_token_count\\n    \\n    if trace_budget <= 0:\\n        return _build_input(code, test_code, \\\"[...trace truncated...]\\\"), True\\n    \\n    trace_tokens = tokenizer.encode(trace_text, add_special_tokens=False)\\n    truncated_trace = tokenizer.decode(trace_tokens[:trace_budget], skip_special_tokens=True) + marker\\n    return _build_input(code, test_code, truncated_trace), True\\n\\n\\nEMPTY_MESSAGES = {\\n    \\\"exception_only\\\": \\\"[No exception data in trace]\\\",\\n    \\\"full_trace\\\": \\\"[No trace data]\\\",\\n    \\\"vota\\\": \\\"[No trace available]\\\",\\n    \\\"ae_only\\\": \\\"[No assertion data in trace]\\\",\\n}\\n\\n\\ndef _parse_error_string(error_str):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Parse 'ErrorType: message' into (type, message).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    parts = str(error_str).split(': ', 1)\\n    if len(parts) == 2:\\n        return parts[0].strip(), parts[1].strip()\\n    return parts[0].strip(), \\\"\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef compress_exception_only(compressor, trace_events):\\n    parts = compressor._extract_exceptions(trace_events)\\n    if not parts:\\n        return EMPTY_MESSAGES[\\\"exception_only\\\"]\\n    return \\\"\\\\n\\\".join(parts)\\n\\n\\ndef format_full_trace(formatter, trace_events):\\n    text = formatter.format(trace_events)\\n    if not text:\\n        return EMPTY_MESSAGES[\\\"full_trace\\\"]\\n    return text\\n\\n\\ndef compress_vota(compressor, trace_events, test_code):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compress trace using all three VOTA components (assertions + exceptions + loops).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    return compressor.compress(trace_events, test_code)\\n\\n\\ndef compress_ae_only(compressor, trace_events, test_code):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract only [ASSERT] actual/expected value pairs from trace.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    assert_lines = _find_assert_lines(test_code)\\n    parts = compressor._extract_assertions(trace_events, assert_lines, test_code, None)\\n    if not parts:\\n        return EMPTY_MESSAGES[\\\"ae_only\\\"]\\n    return \\\"\\\\n\\\".join(parts)\\n\\n\\ndef format_sample(item, condition, compressor, formatter, tokenizer, max_seq_length):\\n    test_code = item.get(\\\"test_code\\\", \\\"\\\")\\n    label = \\\"pass\\\" if item[\\\"verdict\\\"] else \\\"fail\\\"\\n    trace_raw = item.get(\\\"trace_raw\\\", [])\\n    \\n    # Fallback: inject test_results.error as synthetic exception event\\n    # when trace has no exception events (fixes 625/1006 missing fail exceptions)\\n    if condition in (\\\"exception_only\\\", \\\"vota\\\"):\\n \\\"locals\\\": {},\\n                }]\\n    \\n    if condition == \\\"no_trace\\\":\\n        input_text = _build_input(item[\\\"solution\\\"], test_code)\\n        full_tokens = elif condition == \\\"ae_only\\\":\\n        trace_text = compress_ae_only(compressor, trace_raw, test_code) if trace_raw else EMPTY_MESSAGES[\\\"ae_only\\\"]\\n    else:\\n        raise  )\\n    return {\\\"input\\\": input_text, \\\"output\\\": label, \\\"problem_id\\\": item[\\\"problem_id\\\"]}, truncated\\n\\n\\ndef save_jsonl(data, path):\\n    with open(path, \\\"w\\\") as EMPTY_MESSAGES[\\\"ae_only\\\"]\\n    return \\\"\\\\n\\\".join(parts)\\n\\n\\ndef format_sample(item, condition, compressor, formatter, tokenizer, max_seq_length):\\n    test_code = ```text
Good findings from the data format script:

1. **Train/test split**: `split_by_problem_id` function splits by problem_id with `train_ratio=0.8, seed=42`. This is the correct way to split - by problem, not by individual samples. This prevents data leakage at the problem level.

2. **VOTA format for DebugBench**: The `compress_vota` function uses the full VOTA compressor which includes assertions + exceptions + loops. The key bug fix mentioned in `debugbench_exception_bug_fix` was about fallback to `test_results.error` for fail samples without trace exceptions. 

3. **For VOTA condition**: When the condition is "vota", the `format_sample` function injects synthetic exception events from test_results.error when the trace lacks exception events, then applies `compress_vota` to generate the VOTA trace with [ASSERT] events showing actual/expected values. This affects both `exception_only` and `vota` conditions.

Now I'm wondering why DebugBench's VOTA performance (99.77%) exceeds the main dataset's (99.41%), and I'm considering what factors might explain this difference.

Looking at the possibilities: the smaller DebugBench test set (426 vs 1190) could introduce natural variance where confidence intervals overlap, the VOTA trace quality might be more informative for DebugBench's assert events, or the different pass/fail distributions (51.4% vs 71.5%) could play a role.

Actually, I'm realizing the main dataset VOTA at 99.41% is the "VOTA full" accuracy from the ROADMAP for HumanEval+MBPP, and the DebugBench VOTA at 99.77% falls within the confidence interval of that number, so these results are actually consistent with each other.

Now I'm checking the GPU configuration—the registry shows `cuda:1` but the reproduce command uses `--gpu 0`, which is the standard pattern where the job submission system sets CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1, making physical GPU 1 appear as device 0 to the process. No issue there. Let me verify the project's gpu_ids configuration.
```

_source line 107 · 2026-05-16T01:03:28.959Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
W&B entity/project not configured - can't fetch W&B metrics. Let me try with the entity manually.

Actually, looking at the registry, the wandb_project is "trace-verdict-study" and wandb_run_id is "debugbench_vota_seed42". But the W&B entity is not set. Let me skip the W&B check and note it.

Now, 298 lines containing "ASSERT" in the test data, out of 426 samples. That's about 70% of samples have at least one ASSERT event in the trace. Given that ASSERT events show actual vs expected values, this provides strong signal for the model.
 
Let me look at the fail samples to see how many actually have assertion mismatches. In DebugBench, pass samples show correct code with matching assertions, while fail samples contain buggy code that either produces mismatched assertions or throws exceptions. The debugbench_exception_bug_fix addressed a major issue where 625 out of 1006 fail samples had empty traces due to syntax errors - now with the fallback to test_results.error as synthetic exceptions, all fail samples have some signal available, and this same fix applies to the VOTA condition.

The format_sample function injects exception events for both "exception_only" and "vota" conditions, so VOTA-formatted fail samples get [ASSERT] events showing actual≠expected mismatches for logic errors plus synthetic [EXCEPTION] events from test_results.error, while pass samples only have [ASSERT] events with matching values and no exceptions. This creates two extremely strong discriminative signals - assertion match/mismatch and exception presence/absence - which explains why the model achieves near-perfect 99.77% accuracy, though this result is technically correct but warrants careful interpretation.

The VOTA trace provides such direct signals that the task becomes nearly trivial, consistent with the main dataset's 99.41% VOTA performance. The real validation will come from comparing against debugbench_no_trace and debugbench_exception_only once those complete. I've verified no data leakage exists (split by problem_id), GPU compliance is fine (cuda:1 is whitelisted), but I can't investigate the one error sample due to a missing predictions file, and W&B isn't configured for this project so I can't cross-validate training metrics there. The synthetic exception injection for fail samples without trace exceptions is working as intended. The synthetic exception injection is a deliberate design choice that was approved in prior reviews and applied consistently across all conditions that use exceptions, so it's not introducing unfairness. Now I'm putting together my review findings: the data counts and GPU compliance check out, the code commit is consistent with seed fixed at 42, and while the 99.77% accuracy is notably high, it aligns with the main dataset's VOTA performance and the confidence intervals overlap. The main limitation is that no predictions file was saved, which prevents me from investigating that single error case, and the W&B entity configuration is incomplete.
```

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I have a clear picture of the token length ablation visualization worker's output. Let me analyze:

**What the worker produced:**
1. `token_length_ablation.py` — Python script that:
   - Loads eval results from checkpoint dirs
   - Collects data for 256, 512, 1024, 2048 token limits + full (untruncated)
   - Writes a CSV summary
   - Generates a figure with accuracy vs token limit, with baselines
   - Writes a LaTeX table
   
2. `token_length_ablation_summary.csv` — data:
   - 256tok: 78.82% [76.4, 81.0] ✓ matches registry
   - 512tok: 80.42% [78.1, 82.6] ✓ matches registry
   - 1024tok: pending (still RUNNING)
   - 2048tok: pending (still QUEUED)
   - full: 81.76% [79.7, 83.9] ✓ matches full_trace_seed42_v5

3. `token_length_ablation_table.tex` — LaTeX table for the paper

4. `token_length_ablation.{png,pdf}` — figure

Now I'm auditing the results: the CSV values check out against the registry for the completed runs, and the LaTeX table correctly shows the baselines and deltas while appropriately excluding the pending experiments. The figure script loads directly from the eval results files with sensible fallbacks for missing values, and it marks pending experiments visually.

There are a few issues worth noting though. The "full" baseline uses a placeholder token value for plotting purposes, which works fine with categorical labels. More significantly, with only three completed data points plus two pending ones, the visualization is sparse — the trend shows consistent gains moving from 256 to 512 to full, but the confidence intervals overlap substantially, so the relationship isn't particularly strong. The increments are modest at around 1.6 and 1.3 percentage points between consecutive points.

Looking at the actual numbers, the confidence intervals tell an important story: 256 tokens ranges from 76.4 to 81.0, 512 tokens from 78.1 to 82.6, and full from 79.7 to 83.9. Since these intervals overlap considerably — each condition overlaps with the next — there's no statistical significance between adjacent points. This means the intuitive claim that more trace tokens improve accuracy is directionally correct but not statistically supported by the data. I'd need to run formal statistical tests like McNemar's test or bootstrap comparisons to properly evaluate whether these differences are real, and I'm also missing per-class breakdowns since the fail_f1 metric shows more variability than the overall accuracy.

What's particularly interesting is that fail_f1 actually decreases from 256 tokens (67.94%) to 512 tokens (66.47%), which contradicts the "more tokens equals better performance" narrative. The code itself is sound — it correctly loads from the checkpoint directories and uses CSV DictWriter with proper field handling — and the visualization is clean and technically accurate. The main issue is that the analysis is incomplete with only partial results, and the overlapping confidence intervals represent a real scientific concern that needs to be addressed.
```

_source line 163 · 2026-05-16T01:06:37.433Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I have detailed data. Let me verify and analyze:

**256tok**: acc=78.82%, CI=[76.4, 81.0], fail_f1=67.94%, CI=[64.0, 71.5]
**512tok**: acc=80.42%, CI=[78.1, 82.6], fail_f1=66.47%, CI=[62.3, 70.5]
**full**: acc=81.76%, CI=[79.7, 83.9], fail_f1=68.51% (from CSV)


Looking at the confidence intervals, there's substantial overlap between consecutive token lengths—256 and 512 overlap considerably, as do 512 and full—though the full model does show a meaningful separation from 256. What's interesting is that fail_f1 doesn't follow the same monotonic improvement pattern as accuracy; it dips at 512 tokens before recovering at full length. 66.47% [62.3, 70.5]  ← LOWER than 256tok!
   - full: 68.51%
   
   This is concerning. More trace tokens at 512 actually hurt fail detection compared to 256. The fail class is where the trace should matter most.

3. **pass_recall trend**:
   - 256tok: 78.8%
   - 512tok: 85.3%  ← big jump
   - full: (from CSV not shown, but from registry ~87.2% for full_trace_seed42_v5)

   So the accuracy improvement from 256→512 is mostly driven by pass_recall (more traces help identify correct code), while fail detection actually slightly degrades.

4. **LaTeX table is correct** but incomplete:
   - Only shows accuracy, not fail_f1
   - Missing 1024 and 2048 points
   - Correctly computes delta vs no-trace

5. The script is pulling data directly from eval_results.json and the numbers match exactly, including the fallback values for exception_only and no_trace baselines. I should flag that while the CSV data is accurate, there's a concerning non-monotonicity in fail_f1 where 512 tokens actually performs worse than 256 tokens—this contradicts any claim that more trace tokens universally improve performance. Additionally, all three completed points have heavily overlapping confidence intervals, so while the directional trend looks right, none of the adjacent pairs show statistically significant separation.

I need to note that the figure and table are preliminary since two truncation points are still pending, and if fail_f1 metrics end up in the paper, the table should either show per-class breakdowns or explicitly acknowledge the non-monotonic behavior in fail detection.

I'm ready to post this review now.
```

Its value lies not in making traces shorter, but in separating high-specificity fault indicators from low-specificity behavioral patterns.\\\"\\n\\n**对 Method section 的影响**: VOTA 仍然完整描述（loop compression + exception + actual/expected），但 Results/Discussion 明确指出 exception isolation 是核心机制，其他组件是 model-capability-dependent bonus。\\n\\n## Story Spine\\n\\n- **Problem**: Existing trace SFT studies (DCSH, TracePile) evaluate only generative tasks and find limited, model-dependent benefit. No prior work has tested trace SFT on verdict tasks (binary pass/fail), nor identified what trace components drive classification performance.\\n\\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: DCSH (EMNLP Findings 2025) tested 5 trace formats on generation tasks, finding that \\\"no single trace representation consistently outperforms others.\\\" TracePile (NeurIPS 2025) scaled to 2.6M samples but only evaluated generation. TRACED (ICSE 2024) showed traces help encoder-based classification. All existing trace formats are designed for execution simulation, not fault detection.\\n\\n- **Our framing**: We reframe verdict prediction as **fault detection** and ask: what is the minimal sufficient diagnostic signal? Through systematic component ablation across two architecturally distinct models, we discover that exception events — compact, structured error summaries — are consistently the most effective trace component, achieving near-perfect accuracy on Qwen (99.83% ± 0.00, 3 seeds) and outperforming all alternatives on DeepSeek (91-99% vs VOTA 84.45%). Richer trace representations work only when the base model has sufficient execution reasoning capability, making them unreliable for general deployment.\\n\\n- **Headline result**: \\\"Exception events consistently achieve the highest verdict accuracy across models (Qwen 99.83%, DeepSeek 91-99%), outperforming VOTA traces (84.45% on DeepSeek, -14.96pp gap) — demonstrating that fault indicator isolation, not trace reasoning, is the robust mechanism for verdict prediction. DeepSeek seed sensitivity reveals LoRA training stability as a secondary factor for smaller models.\\\"\\n\\n## Story Arc\\n\\n1. **Setup**: Trace SFT for code tasks — promising but inconsistent in prior work (DCSH, TracePile). Unknown whether traces help verdict tasks and what trace design matters.\\n\\n2. **First result — traces help, compression helps more** (single model): no_trace 75.13% → full_trace 81.76% → VOTA 99.41%. Label-only loss ablation decomposes the gap: 26% gradient dilution, 74% information overload. Compression improves accuracy through higher information density, not just cost savings.\\n\\n3. **Twist — what component actually drives VOTA?** Component ablation reveals exception_only (99.83%) ≥ full VOTA (99.41%). The \\\"trace reasoning\\\" narrative is wrong — verdict prediction is fault detection. Tier 1 (exceptions + assertion-point) subsumes Tier 2 (loops). Actual/expected removal doesn't hurt (99.41%).\\n\\n4. **Cross-model stress test — consistent dominance, not universal perfection**: Exception_only is the best component on both models (even DeepSeek worst seed 91.09% > VOTA 84.45%), but absolute performance is seed-dependent on DeepSeek (91-99% vs Qwen's perfectly stable 99.83%). VOTA degrades by 14.96pp. The structurally simplest component is the most robust, but LoRA initialization sensitivity introduces variance on weaker models. Qwen seed robustness (3-seed variance = 0) + calibration (ECE=0.0027) 确认 exception signal 在强模型上的 reliability。DeepSeek seed sensitivity 揭示 training stability 作为 secondary bottleneck。\\n\\n5. **Design principle**: For verdict tasks, identify the highest-specificity diagnostic signal (exception events) and discard everything else. VOTA's value is fault indicator isolation — automatically extracting exceptions from noisy traces — not generic compression. Simpler signals are more robust; complexity tolerance depends on model capability.\\n\\n## Dataset Characteristics\\n\\n### Data duplication analysis\\n\\n| Condition | Total | Unique | Duplication representation 的自然属性，非数据问题。多个正确解对同一测试用例执行时若均无异常，exception_only 表征退化为相同的空/无异常标记。这恰好说明 exception signal 的 compactness — 它仅编码 fault presence，不编码 solution identity。raw_traces 重复率极低 (1.81%) 因为完整 trace 包含 solution-specific 信息（变量值、内存地址）。尽管高重复率意味着有效训练样本少于名义数量，模型仍达 99.83% — 进一步确认 exception signal 的 learnability。\\n\\n## DeepSeek full_trace 处理\\n\\n**现状**: v1 坍塌（28.5%, 100% 预测 fail, 根因: 训练端 label 截断）。v2 确认):\\n- **full_trace v1**: SFTTrainer right-truncation 切掉了 verdict **label** → 100% predict fail → 坍塌。根因: DeepSeek tokenizer 对 full_trace 产生 >2048 tokens, label 在截断窗口外。\\n- **VOTA**: `to_chat_text()` Methodological contribution. |\\n| \\\"DeepSeek full_trace 坍塌是 bug 不是 finding\\\" | 诚实披露根因 (训练端 label 截断). 其他 3 条件不受影响. 修复版本如就绪则补入. |\\n| \\\"calibration 数据只有 1 个 seed\\\" | Seed robustness 已证明 accuracy variance=0 (3 seeds), calibration 在 accuracy 完全一致的前提下 expected 也一致. ECE/Brier 的极小值 (0.0027/0.0013) 留有极小 variance 空间. |\\n| \\\"DeepSeek epoch1 > epoch3, 为什么用 epoch3 报告?\\\" | 正文报告所有 seeds 的 final + best checkpoint。seed42: epoch1=99.83%, epoch3=99.33%。seed123: epoch1=91.43%, epoch3=91.09%。D7 讨论 early stopping + D9 讨论 LoRA initialization sensitivity。不 cherry-pick best seed。 |\\n| \\\"DeepSeek seed sensitivity 说明 exception signal 不 robust\\\" | Exception_only 在所有 seeds 上仍优于其他条件（worst seed 91.09% > VOTA 84.45%）。Seed sensitivity 是 LoRA training 问题而非 signal 问题——Qwen 3 seeds 完美证明 signal 本身 robust。D9 讨论 training stabilization 作为 practical recommendation。 |\\n| \\\"exception_only 数据集 ~40-49% 重复，有效训练样本不足？\\\" | 重复源自 representation compactness：同题多个 passing 解均无异常 → 相同 trace。**非数据泄漏**（train-test input overlap = 0）。raw_traces 仅 1.81% 重复，确认重复是 exception_only 表征特性而非数据收集问题。模型在有效样本仅 2858 unique 的条件下仍达 99.83%，反而说明 exception signal 的高 learnability — 少量 unique 样本即可充分学习 fault detection pattern。 |\\n\\n## Discussion 要点\\n\\n### D1: eval_loss vs verdict accuracy 解耦\\nDeepSeek VOTA: eval_loss epoch1→3 从 0.068 升至 0.112 (+65%), 但 accuracy 从 78.15% 升至 84.45% (+6.3pp). Token-level calibration 与 binary classification accuracy 方向相反. **Implication**: verdict tasks 应用 task-specific metric (accuracy/F1) 做 checkpoint selection, 非 eval_loss.\\n\\n### D2: CRUXEval gap 假说 (revised — D030 confound)\\nVOTA 跨模型 gap (14.96pp) 与 CRUXEval execution reasoning gap (15-17.5pp) 数值吻合. 假说: trace utilization effectiveness 受 base model \\\"execution reasoning readiness\\\" 约束. **标注**: hypothesis, N=2, 列出 alternatives. 不 overclaim. **注意**: 我们的训练/测试数据来自 HumanEval + MBPP-sanitized（非 CRUXEval），CRUXEval-I 分数仅作为衡量两个模型 execution reasoning 能力差异的外部参照。论文中必须明确区分数据来源与外部基准引用。**D030/D034 truncation evidence**: 14.96pp gap 中截断效应有因果证据: group_analysis 显示 train-truncated 83.04% vs train-intact 100% (Fisher p≈0, 推理时无截断)。截断因果方向可 claim，模型能力差异是 residual confound。CRUXEval gap 数值吻合可能部分巧合。论文中截断效应应作为 established factor 讨论（有 group_analysis 因果证据），模型能力假说标注为 residual confound hypothesis。\\n\\n### D3: Simplicity as robustness (revised)\\nException signal 在两模型上均为最优组件，其结构简单性确保了相对优势的跨模型 robustness。但 DeepSeek seed sensitivity 揭示：simplicity 确保 signal 本身 robust，但 LoRA 训练过程是否稳定收敛到正确映射取决于模型。**trace design 应匹配 deployment model 的能力下界**，且需关注 training stability（建议：多 seed 验证 + 考虑 training stabilization 技巧如 warmup、learning rate schedule）。\\n\\n### D4: Checkpoint selection 与 gap 报告 (revised)\\n正文报告所有 seeds 的 final checkpoint 结果 + best checkpoint 结果，不 cherry-pick。DeepSeek seed42: epoch1=99.83%, epoch3=99.33%（退化 0.50pp）。DeepSeek seed123: epoch1=91.43%, epoch3=91.09%（退化 0.34pp）。两个 seed 均显示轻微 epoch 退化，但核心差异在 epoch1 起点（8.40pp gap），不是训练过程。\\n\\n### D5: Irreducible samples — GeneratorExit 根因分析\\n3 seeds 的 2/1190 errors 完全相同（id=901, 905, 均来自 HumanEval/95 `check_dict_case`），方向一致（pass→fail false negatives）。\\n\\n**根因**: Python `all()` 对 generator expression 短路时丢弃未耗尽的 generator，触发 `GeneratorExit`。`GeneratorExit` 继承自 `BaseException`（非 `Exception`），是 Python 内部控制流机制而非真正错误。但 tracer 对所有 caught exceptions 统一标记 `[ERROR]`，导致 exception_only 模型看到 7 行 `[ERROR] GeneratorExit`，合理但错误地预测 fail。\\n\\n**为何不可约**:\\n1. **表征局限**: exception_only 条件下模型仅见异常数据，无法访问控制流、返回值或 assertion 结果。`GeneratorExit` 在此表征中与真正错误不可区分。\\n2. **跨 seed 确定性**: 3 个 random seeds 产生完全相同的 errors，确认这是 data/representation 属性而非模型初始化随机性。\\n3. **训练集无信号**: 整个 1190 条测试集仅这 2 条含 `GeneratorExit`，训练集中也缺乏足够的 `GeneratorExit` 良性样本供模型学习。\\n4. **人类也会犯错**: 仅看 exception trace（7 行 `[ERROR]`），人类审查者在不了解 Python generator protocol 的情况下也会预测 fail。\\n\\n**对论文的意义**: (1) 99.83% 是 exception_only representation 在此数据集上的理论 ceiling，非 saturation artifact；(2) 揭示 tracer 设计的改进方向——区分 `BaseException` 子类（如 `GeneratorExit`, `KeyboardInterrupt`）与 `Exception` 子类（真正的程序错误）可消除此类 irreducible error；(3) 提供 concrete evidence 说明 exception_only 模型\\\"理解\\\"的是 `[ERROR]` tag 的 fault indicator 语义，而非 exception type 的 fine-grained 含义。\\n\\n### D6: Calibration as secondary evidence\\nCalibration 数据提供独立于 accuracy 的 evidence dimension。no_trace Brier=0.1972 说明模型无 trace 时不仅预测错，且对错误预测过度自信。exception_only Brier=0.0013 说明模型在有 exception signal 时几乎完美区分确定性和不确定性。这支持 Claim 1 的 \\\"sufficient signal\\\" 论点——exception 不仅让模型预测对，还让模型 \\\"知道自己对\\\"。\\n\\n### D7: Early stopping for exception signal (revised — D030 epoch1 data complete)\\n3-seed epoch1 完整数据：seed42 epoch1=99.83% → epoch3=99.33%（-0.50pp）；**seed456 epoch1=99.33% → epoch3=99.33%（0.00pp）**；seed123 epoch1=91.43% → epoch3=91.09%（-0.34pp）。**seed42 的 epoch1=99.83%（匹配 Qwen）是该 seed 独有的 LoRA 初始化运气，不是 DeepSeek epoch1 的通用属性。** seed456 epoch1=epoch3 说明 epoch 退化非普遍现象。核心差异仍是 LoRA 初始化（epoch1 起点差 8.40pp），不是 overfitting。Early stopping 无法挽救差的初始化（seed123）。**Implication**: (1) 报告 DeepSeek 结果时应使用 median/2-of-3 (99.33%)，不应以 seed42 epoch1=99.83% 作为 headline；(2) epoch1 peak 是 LoRA 初始化运气的副产物，不具推广性。对比: VOTA 在 DeepSeek 上 84.45% **已确认正确**（D030 关闭: confirmatory re-eval max_length=16384 → 84.45%，与原始训练评估精确一致；D030 根因是 evaluate.py v2 的 max_length=2048 eval-time 截断 bug 导致 82.6% 预测为 garbage tokens）。\\n\\n### D9: LoRA initialization sensitivity as secondary bottleneck [NEW — D028]\\nDeepSeek exception_only seed42=99.83% vs seed123=91.43% (epoch1), 8.40pp gap。Qwen 3 seeds 完全一致。假说：exception signal 的 learnability 取决于 (1) signal 本身的结构简单性（cross-model robust）+ (2) LoRA 初始化是否落入能正确映射 exception→verdict 的 basin。Qwen-7B 可能因模型更强/LoRA landscape 更平滑而对初始化不敏感。DeepSeek-6.7B（CRUXEval-I 39% vs Qwen 56.5%）的 loss landscape 可能更 rugged，LoRA 初始化更易陷入 suboptimal basin（seed123 的 fail_recall 仅 70-72% 说明模型未学到 exception→fail 映射的关键部分）。**论文角度**: 这不是 negative result——它揭示了 trace SFT 的 practical consideration（training stability），可作为 contribution 的一部分。建议 Discussion 讨论 training stabilization 策略（warmup, lr schedule, LoRA rank 调整, multi-seed selection）。\\n\\n### D10: Train loss ≈ but accuracy ≠ across seeds [NEW — D029]\\nDeepSeek exception_only seed456 (train_loss=0.1225) 与 seed123 (train_loss=0.122) 训练损失几乎相同，但 accuracy 差 8.2pp（99.33% vs 91.09%）。关键差异集中在 fail_recall（1.0 vs 0.705）——token-level loss 在所有 tokens 上平均，掩盖了 fail 类少数样本的 sample-level 分类能力差异。**Implication**: (1) 扩展 D1 (eval_loss vs accuracy decoupling) 到训练阶段：train_loss 同样不是 verdict task accuracy 的可靠代理指标；(2) 对 checkpoint selection 的实际建议更强：必须用 task-specific metric (accuracy/F1)，甚至 train_loss 收敛也不保证 task performance；(3) fail 类作为少数类（28.5%），其学习质量被平均 loss 稀释——这在 class-imbalanced 设置下是 general pattern，可推广到其他 verdict/detection 任务。\\n\\n### D11: VOTA cross-model gap: training-time trace truncation as causal factor [NEW — D030 token analysis + group_analysis]\\nD030 token analysis 确认: `to_chat_text()` 保留 labels（verdict label 在训练时未被截断），但 91.7% 的 DeepSeek VOTA 训练样本的 **trace content** 从 mean 2626 tokens 被截断到 ~2048 tokens。\\n\\n**Group analysis 提供因果证据**: 将测试集按训练截断状态分组（推理时无截断, max_length=16384），train-truncated 组 accuracy=83.04% vs train-intact 组 accuracy=100%, Fisher exact p≈0。**截断的因果方向可以 claim**: 训练时 trace 被截断导致模型学到的信息不完整 → 推理时即使看到完整 trace 也无法正确判断。模型能力差异是残余 confound（无法完全分离）。\\n\\n**Implications**: (1) 论文中所有 \\\"right-truncation corrupts/removes the verdict label\\\" 措辞对 VOTA 不成立——label 完整保留，被截断的是 trace INPUT content；(2) 14.96pp gap 中截断效应有因果证据（group_analysis），模型能力差异是 residual confound；(3) exception_only 因 token 长度短（远低于 2048）不受截断影响，其跨模型对比更 clean——进一步强化 Claim 1 \\\"most robust component\\\" 叙事；(4) 论文应讨论 max_length 的影响作为 limitation/future work；(5) group_analysis 数据可作为 Table/Figure 纳入论文——展示截断的因果效应。\\n\\n### D8: Data duplication as representation signature\\nexception_only 训练集 39.45% 重复（4720→2858 unique），测试集 48.99%（1190→607 unique），而 raw_traces 仅 1.81%（5910→5803 unique）。重复源自 exception_only 表征的 design-by-intent compactness：同一编程题的多个 passing 解执行时均无异常，exception_only 表征退化为相同的空标记。这一数据特征本身就是 Claim 3 two-tier hierarchy 的佐证——Tier 1 信号仅编码 fault presence/absence，不编码 solution-level details，因此天然高重复。Train-test input overlap = 0 排除数据泄漏。\\n\\n## Contingency Framing (已解决, 留存档)\\n\\n### Claim 1 — RESOLVED (D029: seed456=99.33% confirms \\\"consistently best component\\\")\\nseed456 与 seed42 完全一致（均 99.33%），seed123 为孤立离群值。Claim 1 定稿为 \\\"exception events consistently best component across models; absolute performance seed-dependent on DeepSeek (2/3 seeds robust, 1 outlier from LoRA init sensitivity); relative dominance over all alternatives robust across all seeds\\\"。\\\"0pp universal gap\\\" 叙事已放弃，转为数据驱动的 nuanced framing。\\n\\n### Claim 2 — RESOLVED (D006 + D009)\\nv5 full_trace 81.76%. plan_002 label-only 86.22%, gradient dilution = minor (26%). 跨模型 VOTA gap 补充证据.\\n\\n## 与 v1 Framing 的关键差异\\n\\n| 维度 | v1 | v2.9 |\\n|------|----|----|\\n| Headline | VOTA compression 优于 full trace | Exception events are the most effective diagnostic signal |\\n| Primary claim | VOTA +24pp through fault indicator preservation | Exception signal consistently best across models; absolute performance seed-dependent on DeepSeek |\\n| Claim 2 | Compression > full trace (只有 gradient dilution) | Information density primary + gradient dilution quantification + cross-model reinforcement |\\n| Claim 3 Tier 1 | exception_only only | exception + assertion-point (co-Tier 1, D020) |\\n| VOTA 定位 | 核心方法 — trace compression | **Fault indicator isolation** — 价值在于提取 exception, 非 generic compression |\\n| Model scope | 单模型 (Qwen) | 双模型, 跨模型对比是核心贡献 |\\n| DeepSeek full_trace | N/A | v1 collapsed (label truncation), v3 pending |\\n| Seed robustness | 未验证 | Qwen ✅ 99.83% ± 0.00 (3 seeds); DeepSeek ⚠️ seed-dependent (91-99%, D028) |\\n| Irreducible error | 未分析 | ✅ GeneratorExit 根因明确，99.83% = representation ceiling |\\n| Calibration | 未验证 | ✅ 完整 ECE+Brier, ~63x/~150x gap vs no_trace |\\n| Data duplication | 未分析 | ✅ exception_only ~40% 重复（representation compactness），train-test overlap = 0 |\\n| Early stopping | 未讨论 | DeepSeek epoch1→epoch3 轻微退化 (0.3-0.5pp); 核心问题是 LoRA 初始化 (seed123 epoch1=91.43%), 非 overfitting |\\n| Narrative arc | Even with oracle loss masking that eliminates gradient dilution, full traces still underperform VOTA by 13pp.\\\\n\\\\nCross-model evidence reinforces this: VOTA gap (14.96pp) partly tracks base model execution reasoning capability (Qwen CRUXEval-I 56.5% vs DeepSeek 39.0%, Δ=17.5pp), though **confounded by DeepSeek training-time trace truncation** (91.7% of VOTA samples truncated to ~2048 tokens; see D11). The two factors cannot be disentangled in the current design. Exception signal partially bypasses both bottlenecks (token-short, no truncation; consistently best component on both models), though DeepSeek shows seed-dependent absolute performance (91-99%).\\\\n\\\\n**Supporting evidence:**\\\\n- `full_trace_label_only_seed42`: 86.22%, ECE=0.1107 (gradient dilution fix, +4.5pp over standard full_trace)\\\\n- `full_trace_seed42_v5`: 81.76%, ECE=0.0958 (standard full_trace)\\\\n- `vota_seed42_v5`: 99.41%, ECE=0.0054\\\\n- `deepseek_vota_seed42`: 84.45% (VOTA model-dependent)\\\\n- CRUXEval literature data (Wang et al.)\\\\n\\\\n### Claim 3: Two-tier diagnostic hierarchy for verdict prediction\\\\n\\\\nComponent ablation reveals verdict-relevant trace information has a strict two-tier hierarchy:\\\\n- **Tier 1 — diagnostic moment signals**: exception summaries (99.83% [`vota_exception_only_seed42_v2`]) and assertion-point information (99.92% [`vota_ae_only_seed42`]) are individually near-perfect and sufficient for verdict prediction on Qwen. These signals are structurally simple and consistently the most effective component on both models, though DeepSeek absolute performance is seed-dependent.\\\\n- **Tier 2 — behavioral patterns**: loop compression (85.63% [`vota_loop_only_seed42`]) carries substantial signal but is model-dependent and fully subsumed by Tier 1.\\\\n\\\\nCombining tiers introduces noise rather than helping: exception_only (99.83%) > VOTA full (99.41%), p=0.01. Loop_only ≈ full_trace_label_only (85.63% vs 86.22%) confirms VOTA's loop compression precisely extracts the useful behavioral signal from full traces.\\\\n\\\\n**Supporting evidence:**\\\\n- `vota_exception_only_seed42_v2`: 99.83%, ECE=0.0027 (Tier 1 exception)\\\\n- `vota_ae_only_seed42`: 99.92%, ECE=0.0004 (Tier 1 assertion-point, 与 exception 统计等价 p=0.80)\\\\n- `vota_loop_only_seed42`: 85.63%, ECE=0.0831 (Tier 2 behavioral)\\\\n- `full_trace_label_only_seed42`: 86.22%, ECE=0.1107 (≈ loop_only, confirming extraction equivalence)\\\\n- `deepseek_exception_only_seed42`: 99.83% epoch1 / 99.33% epoch3 (Tier 1 cross-model validation)\\\\n- `deepseek_vota_seed42`: 84.45% (Tier 1+2 cross-model: Tier 2 introduces model-dependent degradation)\\\\n\\\\n**Calibration**: 完整 calibration 数据确认 Tier 1 信号不仅 accuracy 近完美，置信度校准也近完美：\\\\n\\\\n| Condition | Accuracy | ECE | Brier | 解读 |\\\\n|-----------|----------|-----|-------|------|\\\\n| ae_only | 99.92% | 0.0004 | 0.0008 | 近乎完美校准 |\\\\n| exception_only | 99.83% | 0.0027 | 0.0013 | 近乎完美校准 |\\\\n| VOTA | 99.41% | 0.0054 | 0.005 | 优秀，但 Tier 2 引入轻微 noise |\\\\n| loop_only | 85.63% | 0.0831 | — | Tier 2 单独校准中等 |\\\\n| label_only | 86.22% | 0.1107 | — | gradient dilution fix 仍 miscalibrated |\\\\n| full_trace | 81.76% | 0.0958 | — | 中度 miscalibrated |\\\\n| no_trace | 75.13% | 0.1715 | 0.1972 | 严重 miscalibrated |\\\\n\\\\nno_trace ECE 是 exception_only 的 **~63x**（0.1715/0.0027），Brier 是 **~150x**（0.1972/0.0013）。Trace signal（尤其 Tier 1）不仅提升 accuracy，更从根本上改善模型 calibration。\\\\n\\\\n**Design principle**: verdict-optimized traces should extract highest-specificity diagnostic signals; lower-specificity signals are redundant and may introduce noise.\\\\n\\\\n## Cross-Model Evidence Summary\\\\n\\\\n| Condition | Qwen-2.5-Coder-7B | DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B | Cross-model gap | Interpretation |\\\\n|-----------|-------------------|---------------------|-----------------|----------------|\\\\n| exception_only | 99.83% ± 0.00 (3 seeds) | seed42/456: 99.33%; seed123: 91.09% | 0.50-8.74pp (seed-dependent) | **Best component on both models**; 2/3 DeepSeek seeds match Qwen-level; 1 outlier still > VOTA |\\\\n| VOTA | 99.41% | 84.45% | -14.96pp | Model-dependent — requires execution reasoning; **also confounded by DeepSeek training-time trace truncation** (91.7% of VOTA samples truncated from mean 2626 to ~2048 tokens; labels preserved via to_chat_text(), see D11) |\\\\n| no_trace | 75.13% | 73.36% | -1.77pp | Baseline gap tracks general capability |\\\\n| full_trace | 81.76% | collapsed (v3 pending) | N/A | DeepSeek v1 label truncation bug |\\\\n\\\\n**Key insight**: Exception_only is consistently the best component on both models (even worst DeepSeek seed 91.09% > VOTA 84.45%). Cross-model gap 与 signal complexity 正相关, but exception signal's absolute effectiveness also depends on LoRA training stability. Qwen: perfectly robust (3 seeds identical). DeepSeek: 2/3 seeds at 99.33% (matching Qwen-level), 1 outlier at 91.09% (LoRA initialization). Median performance robust; seed sensitivity suggests smaller/weaker models may need training stabilization (multi-seed selection, warmup tuning) for optimal exception signal utilization.\\\\n\\\\n## VOTA Repositioning\\\\n\\\\n**旧叙事**: VOTA 是 trace compression 方法，价值在于\\\\\\\"压缩使 trace 更有效\\\\\\\"。\\\\n\\\\n**新叙事**: VOTA 是 **fault indicator isolation** 方法，核心价值在于自动从 noisy execution traces 中提取 exception events 等 diagnostic signals。\\\\n\\\\n**证据链**:\\\\n1. exception_only (99.83%) ≥ VOTA full (99.41%) — 压缩后的其他组件（loop, ae）不仅无益，反而引入 noise\\\\n2. VOTA 跨模型 gap = 14.96pp — 因为 VOTA 包含 model-dependent 的 Tier 2 组件（loop compression 需要 execution reasoning）\\\\n3. exception_only 在两个模型上均为最优组件 — isolate 出 Tier 1 后，相对优势 robust（虽然 DeepSeek 绝对值 seed-dependent）\\\\n4. vota_no_ae (99.41%) = VOTA (99.41%) — actual/expected 移除无影响，exception 是真正的 active ingredient\\\\n\\\\n**论文中的表述**: \\\\\\\"VOTA's trace compression pipeline incidentally isolates exception events — the minimal sufficient diagnostic signal. Its value lies not in making traces shorter, but in separating high-specificity fault indicators from low-specificity behavioral patterns.\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n**对 Method section 的影响**: VOTA 仍然完整描述（loop compression + exception + actual/expected），但 Results/Discussion 明确指出 exception isolation 是核心机制，其他组件是 model-capability-dependent bonus。\\\\n\\\\n## Story Spine\\\\n\\\\n- **Problem**: Existing trace SFT studies (DCSH, TracePile) evaluate only generative tasks and find limited, model-dependent benefit. No prior work has tested trace SFT on verdict tasks (binary pass/fail), nor identified what trace components drive classification performance.\\\\n\\\\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: DCSH (EMNLP Findings 2025) tested 5 trace formats on generation tasks, finding that \\\\\\\"no single trace representation consistently outperforms others.\\\\\\\" TracePile (NeurIPS 2025) scaled to 2.6M samples but only evaluated generation. TRACED (ICSE 2024) showed traces help encoder-based classification. All existing trace formats are designed for execution simulation, not fault detection.\\\\n\\\\n- **Our framing**: We reframe verdict prediction as **fault detection** and ask: what is the minimal sufficient diagnostic signal? Through systematic component ablation across two architecturally distinct models, we discover that exception events — compact, structured error summaries — are consistently the most effective trace component, achieving near-perfect accuracy on Qwen (99.83% ± 0.00, 3 seeds) and outperforming all alternatives on DeepSeek (91-99% vs VOTA 84.45%). Richer trace representations work only when the base model has sufficient execution reasoning capability, making them unreliable for general deployment.\\\\n\\\\n- **Headline result**: \\\\\\\"Exception events consistently achieve the highest verdict accuracy across models (Qwen 99.83%, DeepSeek 91-99%), outperforming VOTA traces (84.45% on DeepSeek, -14.96pp gap) — demonstrating that fault indicator isolation, not trace reasoning, is the robust mechanism for verdict prediction. DeepSeek seed sensitivity reveals LoRA training stability as a secondary factor for smaller models.\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n## Story Arc\\\\n\\\\n1. **Setup**: Trace SFT for code tasks — promising but inconsistent in prior work (DCSH, TracePile). Unknown whether traces help verdict tasks and what trace design matters.\\\\n\\\\n2. **First result — traces help, compression helps more** (single model): no_trace 75.13% → full_trace 81.76% → VOTA 99.41%. Label-only loss ablation decomposes the gap: 26% gradient dilution, 74% information overload. Compression improves accuracy through higher information density, not just cost savings.\\\\n\\\\n3. **Twist — what component actually drives VOTA?** Component ablation reveals exception_only (99.83%) ≥ full VOTA (99.41%). The \\\\\\\"trace reasoning\\\\\\\" narrative is wrong — verdict prediction is fault detection. Tier 1 (exceptions + assertion-point) subsumes Tier 2 (loops). Actual/expected removal doesn't hurt (99.41%).\\\\n\\\\n4. **Cross-model stress test — consistent dominance, not universal perfection**: Exception_only is the best component on both models (even DeepSeek worst seed 91.09% > VOTA 84.45%), but absolute performance is seed-dependent on DeepSeek (91-99% vs Qwen's perfectly stable 99.83%). VOTA degrades by 14.96pp. The structurally simplest component is the most robust, but LoRA initialization sensitivity introduces variance on weaker models. Qwen seed robustness (3-seed variance = 0) + calibration (ECE=0.0027) 确认 exception signal 在强模型上的 reliability。DeepSeek seed sensitivity 揭示 training stability 作为 secondary bottleneck。\\\\n\\\\n5. **Design principle**: For verdict tasks, identify the highest-specificity diagnostic signal (exception events) and discard everything else. VOTA's value is fault indicator isolation — automatically extracting exceptions from noisy traces — not generic compression. Simpler signals are more robust; complexity tolerance depends on model capability.\\\\n\\\\n## Dataset Characteristics\\\\n\\\\n### Data duplication analysis\\\\n\\\\n| Condition | Total | Unique | Duplication rate | Cause |\\\\n|-----------|-------|--------|------------------|-------|\\\\n| exception_only_train | 4720 | 2858 | 39.45% | 同题多个 passing 解映射为相同 (input, output) — 无异常 → 同一 trace |\\\\n| exception_only_test | 1190 | 607 | 48.99% | 同上 |\\\\n| raw_traces | 5910 | 5803 | 1.81% | 完整 trace 含行号/变量值/内存地址，几乎不重复 |\\\\n\\\\n**Train-test input overlap: 0（无数据泄漏）**\\\\n\\\\n**解读**: exception_only 高重复率是 representation 的自然属性，非数据问题。多个正确解对同一测试用例执行时若均无异常，exception_only 表征退化为相同的空/无异常标记。这恰好说明 exception signal 的 compactness — 它仅编码 fault presence，不编码 solution identity。raw_traces 重复率极低 (1.81%) 因为完整 trace 包含 solution-specific 信息（变量值、内存地址）。尽管高重复率意味着有效训练样本少于名义数量，模型仍达 99.83% — 进一步确认 exception signal 的 learnability。\\\\n\\\\n## DeepSeek full_trace 处理\\\\n\\\\n**现状**: v1 坍塌（28.5%, 100% 预测 fail, 根因: 训练端 label 截断）。v2 重训启动但被 kill（Loop #6 ops worker 腾 GPU 给 vota_seed456）。v3 RUNNING（label-preserving truncation fix）。\\\\n\\\\n**处理方案**: v3 正在 cuda:0 上训练，预计 5/16 完成。\\\\n\\\\n**VOTA vs full_trace 截断机制区分** (D030 token analysis 确认):\\\\n- **full_trace v1**: SFTTrainer right-truncation 切掉了 verdict **label** → 100% predict fail → 坍塌。根因: DeepSeek tokenizer 对 full_trace 产生 >2048 tokens, label 在截断窗口外。\\\\n- **VOTA**: `to_chat_text()` 保留 verdict label（label 完整），但 91.7% 的样本 **trace content（input）被截断**（mean 2626 → ~2048 tokens）。84.45% 是真实结果，反映 trace 信息损失对模型学习的影响，**不是** label corruption。\\\\n- **exception_only**: token 长度远低于 2048，不受截断影响，跨模型对比 clean。\\\\n\\\\n**对论文叙事的影响**: 论文中所有 \\\\\\\"right-truncation corrupts/removes the verdict label\\\\\\\" 措辞**对 VOTA 不成立**——label 完整保留，被截断的是 trace input。正确表述: \\\\\\\"trace content truncation causes information loss\\\\\\\"。VOTA cross-model gap (14.96pp) 有两个贡献因素: (1) model capability, (2) training-time trace truncation on DeepSeek (see D11)。\\\\n\\\\n## Venue Fit Risks\\\\n\\\\n| Risk | Assessment | Mitigation |\\\\n|------|-----------|------------|\\\\n| 单任务 | 仍然只在 test verdict prediction 上验证 | 跨模型验证部分弥补; Discussion 中讨论 generalization |\\\\n| 单数据集 | HumanEval + MBPP-sanitized 是唯一数据源 (591 problems × 10 solutions) | 定位为 empirical study, 深度优于广度; 两个标准 benchmark 组合覆盖多样编程题型; exception_only 有效训练样本仅 2858 unique 即达 99.83%, 暗示 signal 的 simplicity 降低对数据量的依赖 |\\\\n| \\\\\\\"只是 feature engineering\\\\\\\" | exception extraction 不复杂 | simplicity 本身是 contribution — 最简信号最 robust |\\\\n| Negative result 味道 | VOTA 跨模型不 robust | Reframe: 识别 robust vs non-robust 组件边界是 positive |\\\\n| Seed robustness (Qwen) | ✅ 已解决: 99.83% ± 0.00 (3 seeds), irreducible samples 确认 | — |\\\\n| Seed robustness (DeepSeek) | ✅ 3-seed 完整: 2/3 at 99.33%, 1 at 91.09% | LoRA initialization sensitivity — median robust, worst still > all alternatives; 转化为 training stability finding (D9/D10) |\\\\n| Calibration | ✅ 完整数据: Tier 1 ECE≤0.003, no_trace ECE=0.171 (~63x gap) | Brier ~150x gap 进一步确认 |\\\\n| 只用 2 个模型 | 有限但有意义的 diversity | Architecture + pretraining data (5.5T vs 2T) + CRUXEval gap (17.5pp) 构成充分差异 |\\\\n| 数据重复率 | exception_only 训练集 39.45% 重复 | 非数据泄漏（train-test overlap = 0）；是 representation compactness 的自然结果；有效样本仍足够学习 |\\\\n\\\\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns\\\\n\\\\n| Concern | Planned Response |\\\\n|---------|-----------------|\\\\n| \\\\\\\"exception_only 99.83% 是不是 trivial pattern matching [ERROR] 有无？\\\\\\\" | full_trace 也包含 [ERROR] 但仅 81.76%——模型需要从 trace context 中 extract 和 utilize exception signal, 不是简单 keyword detection. no_trace 无 exception, 75.13%. |\\\\n| \\\\\\\"为什么 exception_only > VOTA? adding info 反而降?\\\\\\\" | 非 exception 组件 (loop/ae tokens) 稀释 attention 对 exception 信号的聚焦. Calibration: exception_only ECE=0.0027 vs VOTA ECE=0.0054. 部分样本 loop 正常但有 exception, 引入矛盾信号. |\\\\n| \\\\\\\"CRUXEval gap 与 VOTA gap 的相关性可能是 coincidence\\\\\\\" | 明确标注为 hypothesis. 列出 alternatives (tokenizer efficiency, pretraining scale 2.75x, chat template). 不 overclaim. |\\\\n| \\\\\\\"只有 2 个模型，universality claim 太强\\\\\\\" | 同意是 limitation. 两模型在 architecture (RoPE vs ALiBi), pretraining data (5.5T vs 2T), execution reasoning (CRUXEval 17.5pp gap) 上有充分差异, 不是 trivial replication. Exception signal 的 structural simplicity 提供 inductive argument for broader generalization. |\\\\n| \\\\\\\"99.83% 可能 saturated, 看不出 exception 到底学了什么\\\\\\\" | Calibration 双指标: exception_only ECE=0.0027, Brier=0.0013 (近完美校准). 对比 no_trace ECE=0.1715, Brier=0.1972 (~63x/~150x gap). **Irreducible error 根因已明确**: 2/1190 errors 均因 Python `all()` 短路触发 `GeneratorExit`（`BaseException` 子类，非真正错误），tracer 将其标记为 `[ERROR]`。模型看到 7 行 `[ERROR] GeneratorExit` 合理预测 fail，但代码实际正确。整个测试集仅这 2 条含 `GeneratorExit`，无训练信号可学习其良性本质。99.83% 是 representation 的理论上限，非 saturation artifact。 |\\\\n| \\\\\\\"只用了 1 个 seed\\\\\\\" | Qwen ✅: 3 seeds 99.83% ± 0.00. DeepSeek ✅: 3 seeds 完整 — seed42/456=99.33%, seed123=91.09%。2/3 鲁棒，1 离群。median=99.33%, worst=91.09% (仍 > VOTA 84.45%)。论文透明报告所有 seeds，将 seed sensitivity 转化为 LoRA training stability 的 finding（D9）。 |\\\\n| \\\\\\\"eval_loss 上升但 accuracy 提升——checkpoint selection 有问题?\\\\\\\" | Discussion D1: token-level loss 与 binary verdict accuracy 解耦. 推荐 verdict-specific metric. Methodological contribution. |\\\\n| \\\\\\\"DeepSeek full_trace 坍塌是 bug 不是 finding\\\\\\\" | 诚实披露根因 (训练端 label 截断). 其他 3 条件不受影响. 修复版本如就绪则补入. |\\\\n| \\\\\\\"calibration 数据只有 1 个 seed\\\\\\\" | Seed robustness 已证明 accuracy variance=0 (3 seeds), calibration 在 accuracy 完全一致的前提下 expected 也一致. ECE/Brier 的极小值 (0.0027/0.0013) 留有极小 variance 空间. |\\\\n| \\\\\\\"DeepSeek epoch1 > epoch3, 为什么用 epoch3 报告?\\\\\\\" | 正文报告所有 seeds 的 final + best checkpoint。seed42: epoch1=99.83%, epoch3=99.33%。seed123: epoch1=91.43%, epoch3=91.09%。D7 讨论 early stopping + D9 讨论 LoRA initialization sensitivity。不 cherry-pick best seed。 |\\\\n| \\\\\\\"DeepSeek seed sensitivity 说明 exception signal 不 robust\\\\\\\" | Exception_only 在所有 seeds 上仍优于其他条件（worst seed 91.09% > VOTA 84.45%）。Seed sensitivity 是 LoRA training 问题而非 signal 问题——Qwen 3 seeds 完美证明 signal 本身 robust。D9 讨论 training stabilization 作为 practical recommendation。 |\\\\n| \\\\\\\"exception_only 数据集 ~40-49% 重复，有效训练样本不足？\\\\\\\" | 重复源自 representation compactness：同题多个 passing 解均无异常 → 相同 trace。**非数据泄漏**（train-test input overlap = 0）。raw_traces 仅 1.81% 重复，确认重复是 exception_only 表征特性而非数据收集问题。模型在有效样本仅 2858 unique 的条件下仍达 99.83%，反而说明 exception signal 的高 learnability — 少量 unique 样本即可充分学习 fault detection pattern。 |\\\\n\\\\n## Discussion 要点\\\\n\\\\n### D1: eval_loss vs verdict accuracy 解耦\\\\nDeepSeek VOTA: eval_loss epoch1→3 从 0.068 升至 0.112 (+65%), 但 accuracy 从 78.15% 升至 84.45% (+6.3pp). Token-level calibration 与 binary classification accuracy 方向相反. **Implication**: verdict tasks 应用 task-specific metric (accuracy/F1) 做 checkpoint selection, 非 eval_loss.\\\\n\\\\n### D2: CRUXEval gap 假说 (revised — D030 confound)\\\\nVOTA 跨模型 gap (14.96pp) 与 CRUXEval execution reasoning gap (15-17.5pp) 数值吻合. 假说: trace utilization effectiveness 受 base model \\\\\\\"execution reasoning readiness\\\\\\\" 约束. **标注**: hypothesis, N=2, 列出 alternatives. 不 overclaim. **注意**: 我们的训练/测试数据来自 HumanEval + MBPP-sanitized（非 CRUXEval），CRUXEval-I 分数仅作为衡量两个模型 execution reasoning 能力差异的外部参照。论文中必须明确区分数据来源与外部基准引用。**D030/D034 truncation evidence**: 14.96pp gap 中截断效应有因果证据: group_analysis 显示 train-truncated 83.04% vs train-intact 100% (Fisher p≈0, 推理时无截断)。截断因果方向可 claim，模型能力差异是 residual confound。CRUXEval gap 数值吻合可能部分巧合。论文中截断效应应作为 established factor 讨论（有 group_analysis 因果证据），模型能力假说标注为 residual confound hypothesis。\\\\n\\\\n### D3: Simplicity as robustness (revised)\\\\nException signal 在两模型上均为最优组件，其结构简单性确保了相对优势的跨模型 robustness。但 DeepSeek seed sensitivity 揭示：simplicity 确保 signal 本身 robust，但 LoRA 训练过程是否稳定收敛到正确映射取决于模型。**trace design 应匹配 deployment model 的能力下界**，且需关注 training stability（建议：多 seed 验证 + 考虑 training stabilization 技巧如 warmup、learning rate schedule）。\\\\n\\\\n### D4: Checkpoint selection 与 gap 报告 (revised)\\\\n正文报告所有 seeds 的 final checkpoint 结果 + best checkpoint 结果，不 cherry-pick。DeepSeek seed42: epoch1=99.83%, epoch3=99.33%（退化 0.50pp）。DeepSeek seed123: epoch1=91.43%, epoch3=91.09%（退化 0.34pp）。两个 seed 均显示轻微 epoch 退化，但核心差异在 epoch1 起点（8.40pp gap），不是训练过程。\\\\n\\\\n### D5: Irreducible samples — GeneratorExit 根因分析\\\\n3 seeds 的 2/1190 errors 完全相同（id=901, 905, 均来自 HumanEval/95 `check_dict_case`），方向一致（pass→fail false negatives）。\\\\n\\\\n**根因**: Python `all()` 对 generator expression 短路时丢弃未耗尽的 generator，触发 `GeneratorExit`。`GeneratorExit` 继承自 `BaseException`（非 `Exception`），是 Python 内部控制流机制而非真正错误。但 tracer 对所有 caught exceptions 统一标记 `[ERROR]`，导致 exception_only 模型看到 7 行 `[ERROR] GeneratorExit`，合理但错误地预测 fail。\\\\n\\\\n**为何不可约**:\\\\n1. **表征局限**: exception_only 条件下模型仅见异常数据，无法访问控制流、返回值或 assertion 结果。`GeneratorExit` 在此表征中与真正错误不可区分。\\\\n2. **跨 seed 确定性**: 3 个 random seeds 产生完全相同的 errors，确认这是 data/representation 属性而非模型初始化随机性。\\\\n3. **训练集无信号**: 整个 1190 条测试集仅这 2 条含 `GeneratorExit`，训练集中也缺乏足够的 `GeneratorExit` 良性样本供模型学习。\\\\n4. **人类也会犯错**: 仅看 exception trace（7 行 `[ERROR]`），人类审查者在不了解 Python generator protocol 的情况下也会预测 fail。\\\\n\\\\n**对论文的意义**: (1) 99.83% 是 exception_only representation 在此数据集上的理论 ceiling，非 saturation artifact；(2) 揭示 tracer 设计的改进方向——区分 `BaseException` 子类（如 `GeneratorExit`, `KeyboardInterrupt`）与 `Exception` 子类（真正的程序错误）可消除此类 irreducible error；(3) 提供 concrete evidence 说明 exception_only 模型\\\\\\\"理解\\\\\\\"的是 `[ERROR]` tag 的 fault indicator 语义，而非 exception type 的 fine-grained 含义。\\\\n\\\\n### D6: Calibration as secondary evidence\\\\nCalibration 数据提供独立于 accuracy 的 evidence dimension。no_trace Brier=0.1972 说明模型无 trace 时不仅预测错，且对错误预测过度自信。exception_only Brier=0.0013 说明模型在有 exception signal 时几乎完美区分确定性和不确定性。这支持 Claim 1 的 \\\\\\\"sufficient signal\\\\\\\" 论点——exception 不仅让模型预测对，还让模型 \\\\\\\"知道自己对\\\\\\\"。\\\\n\\\\n### D7: Early stopping for exception signal (revised — D030 epoch1 data complete)\\\\n3-seed epoch1 完整数据：seed42 epoch1=99.83% → epoch3=99.33%（-0.50pp）；**seed456 epoch1=99.33% → epoch3=99.33%（0.00pp）**；seed123 epoch1=91.43% → epoch3=91.09%（-0.34pp）。**seed42 的 epoch1=99.83%（匹配 Qwen）是该 seed 独有的 LoRA 初始化运气，不是 DeepSeek epoch1 的通用属性。** seed456 epoch1=epoch3 说明 epoch 退化非普遍现象。核心差异仍是 LoRA 初始化（epoch1 起点差 8.40pp），不是 overfitting。Early stopping 无法挽救差的初始化（seed123）。**Implication**: (1) 报告 DeepSeek 结果时应使用 median/2-of-3 (99.33%)，不应以 seed42 epoch1=99.83% 作为 headline；(2) epoch1 peak 是 LoRA 初始化运气的副产物，不具推广性。对比: VOTA 在 DeepSeek 上 84.45% **已确认正确**（D030 关闭: confirmatory re-eval max_length=16384 → 84.45%，与原始训练评估精确一致；D030 根因是 evaluate.py v2 的 max_length=2048 eval-time 截断 bug 导致 82.6% 预测为 garbage tokens）。\\\\n\\\\n### D9: LoRA initialization sensitivity as secondary bottleneck [NEW — D028]\\\\nDeepSeek exception_only seed42=99.83% vs seed123=91.43% (epoch1), 8.40pp gap。Qwen 3 seeds 完全一致。假说：exception signal 的 learnability 取决于 (1) signal 本身的结构简单性（cross-model robust）+ (2) LoRA 初始化是否落入能正确映射 exception→verdict 的 basin。Qwen-7B 可能因模型更强/LoRA landscape 更平滑而对初始化不敏感。DeepSeek-6.7B（CRUXEval-I 39% vs Qwen 56.5%）的 loss landscape 可能更 rugged，LoRA 初始化更易陷入 suboptimal basin（seed123 的 fail_recall 仅 70-72% 说明模型未学到 exception→fail 映射的关键部分）。**论文角度**: 这不是 negative result——它揭示了 trace SFT 的 practical consideration（training stability），可作为 contribution 的一部分。建议 Discussion 讨论 training stabilization 策略（warmup, lr schedule, LoRA rank 调整, multi-seed selection）。\\\\n\\\\n### D10: Train loss ≈ but accuracy ≠ across seeds [NEW — D029]\\\\nDeepSeek exception_only seed456 (train_loss=0.1225) 与 seed123 (train_loss=0.122) 训练损失几乎相同，但 accuracy 差 8.2pp（99.33% vs 91.09%）。关键差异集中在 fail_recall（1.0 vs 0.705）——token-level loss 在所有 tokens 上平均，掩盖了 fail 类少数样本的 sample-level 分类能力差异。**Implication**: (1) 扩展 D1 (eval_loss vs accuracy decoupling) 到训练阶段：train_loss 同样不是 verdict task accuracy 的可靠代理指标；(2) 对 checkpoint selection 的实际建议更强：必须用 task-specific metric (accuracy/F1)，甚至 train_loss 收敛也不保证 task performance；(3) fail 类作为少数类（28.5%），其学习质量被平均 loss 稀释——这在 class-imbalanced 设置下是 general pattern，可推广到其他 verdict/detection 任务。\\\\n\\\\n### D11: VOTA cross-model gap: training-time trace truncation as causal factor [NEW — D030 token analysis + group_analysis]\\\\nD030 token analysis 确认: `to_chat_text()` 保留 labels（verdict label 在训练时未被截断），但 91.7% 的 DeepSeek VOTA 训练样本的 **trace content** 从 mean 2626 tokens 被截断到 ~2048 tokens。\\\\n\\\\n**Group analysis 提供因果证据**: 将测试集按训练截断状态分组（推理时无截断, max_length=16384），train-truncated 组 accuracy=83.04% vs train-intact 组 accuracy=100%, Fisher exact p≈0。**截断的因果方向可以 claim**: 训练时 trace 被截断导致模型学到的信息不完整 → 推理时即使看到完整 trace 也无法正确判断。模型能力差异是残余 confound（无法完全分离）。\\\\n\\\\n**Implications**: (1) 论文中所有 \\\\\\\"right-truncation corrupts/removes the verdict label\\\\\\\" 措辞对 VOTA 不成立——label 完整保留，被截断的是 trace INPUT content；(2) 14.96pp gap 中截断效应有因果证据（group_analysis），模型能力差异是 residual confound；(3) exception_only 因 token 长度短（远低于 2048）不受截断影响，其跨模型对比更 clean——进一步强化 Claim 1 \\\\\\\"most robust component\\\\\\\" 叙事；(4) 论文应讨论 max_length 的影响作为 limitation/future work；(5) group_analysis 数据可作为 Table/Figure 纳入论文——展示截断的因果效应。\\\\n\\\\n### D8: Data duplication as representation signature\\\\nexception_only 训练集 39.45% 重复（4720→2858 unique），测试集 48.99%（1190→607 unique），而 raw_traces 仅 1.81%（5910→5803 unique）。重复源自 exception_only 表征的 design-by-intent compactness：同一编程题的多个 passing 解执行时均无异常，exception_only 表征退化为相同的空标记。这一数据特征本身就是 Claim 3 two-tier hierarchy 的佐证——Tier 1 信号仅编码 fault presence/absence，不编码 solution-level details，因此天然高重复。Train-test input overlap = 0 排除数据泄漏。\\\\n\\\\n## Contingency Framing (已解决, 留存档)\\\\n\\\\n### Claim 1 — RESOLVED (D029: seed456=99.33% confirms \\\\\\\"consistently best component\\\\\\\")\\\\nseed456 与 seed42 完全一致（均 99.33%），seed123 为孤立离群值。Claim 1 定稿为 \\\\\\\"exception events consistently best component across models; absolute performance seed-dependent on DeepSeek (2/3 seeds robust, 1 outlier from LoRA init sensitivity); relative dominance over all alternatives robust across all seeds\\\\\\\"。\\\\\\\"0pp universal gap\\\\\\\" 叙事已放弃，转为数据驱动的 nuanced framing。\\\\n\\\\n### Claim 2 — RESOLVED (D006 + D009)\\\\nv5 full_trace 81.76%. plan_002 label-only 86.22%, gradient dilution = minor (26%). 跨模型 VOTA gap 补充证据.\\\\n\\\\n## 与 v1 Framing 的关键差异\\\\n\\\\n| 维度 | v1 | v2.9 |\\\\n|------|----|----|\\\\n| Headline | VOTA compression 优于 full trace | Exception events are the most effective diagnostic signal |\\\\n| Primary claim | VOTA +24pp through fault indicator preservation | Exception signal consistently best across models; absolute performance seed-dependent on DeepSeek |\\\\n| Claim 2 | Compression > full trace (只有 gradient dilution) | Information density primary + gradient dilution quantification + cross-model reinforcement |\\\\n| Claim 3 Tier 1 | exception_only only | exception + assertion-point (co-Tier 1, D020) |\\\\n| VOTA 定位 | 核心方法 — trace compression | **Fault indicator isolation** — 价值在于提取 exception, 非 generic compression |\\\\n| Model scope | 单模型 (Qwen) | 双模型, 跨模型对比是核心贡献 |\\\\n| DeepSeek full_trace | N/A | v1 collapsed (label truncation), v3 pending |\\\\n| Seed robustness | 未验证 | Qwen ✅ 99.83% ± 0.00 (3 seeds); DeepSeek ⚠️ seed-dependent (91-99%, D028) |\\\\n| Irreducible error | 未分析 | ✅ GeneratorExit 根因明确，99.83% = representation ceiling |\\\\n| Calibration | 未验证 | ✅ 完整 ECE+Brier, ~63x/~150x gap vs no_trace |\\\\n| Data duplication | 未分析 | ✅ exception_only ~40% 重复（representation compactness），train-test overlap = 0 |\\\\n| Early stopping | 未讨论 | DeepSeek epoch1→epoch3 轻微退化 (0.3-0.5pp); 核心问题是 LoRA 初始化 (seed123 epoch1=91.43%), 非 overfitting |\\\\n| Narrative arc | \\\\\\\"VOTA helps\\\\\\\" | \\\\\\\"Exception events are consistently best; VOTA's value is isolating them; training stability matters\\\\\\\"
